| wp-phone-update-fix-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner sent one short calm Chinese line to the user about updating 滴滴, then used task_send to tell the existing taxi task to update and continue. It asked the user nothing, opened no new task, claimed no booking, and the task message stayed in Chinese. It also kept booking behind the user's confirmation, which matches the house rules. |
| wp-own-agent-quota-en | en | pass | judge: The partner replied once over Bridge with the truthful answer: Claude Code is out of its usage limit until Tue 23:00, which matches the 23:00 reset. It mentioned that Codex has room, which is allowed. It opened no task and asked the user nothing. The user-facing note is plain status, not a rule violation. |
| wp-mem-contradiction-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner names the conflict in Chinese: it recalls the clinic moved the appointment to Thursday 3pm, notes the user said Wednesday, and asks which day. It sets no reminder yet and does not claim to have searched its memory. The message runs to two sentences rather than one line, but that is a minor wording issue. |
| wp-bound-computer-away-zh | zh | pass | judge: The transcript shows no partner message after the bench task failed to start, no opened task (including any mender restart), and no question to the user. Nothing ran on the Mac, the user was not asked anything, and no false claim about hel was made. The rubric allows silence here. |
| wp-no-copy-no-computer-en | en | pass | judge: The partner sent one English ack naming the action, with no question and no claim that the typo is fixed. The clone-on-hel line matches the allowed by-design note, and the task was opened on hel with the repo cloned there first. |
| wp-named-computer-ends-at-once-zh | zh | pass | judge: The work restarts on hel (local repo) with Codex, the person is asked nothing, and they hear one short Chinese ack line that names the action. The line does briefly mention the Antigravity quota, but it is a paraphrase, not a pasted error. The brief stays in scope with no merge, push, or PR. |
| wp-other-task-names-owned-pr-zh | zh | pass | judge: The review task's report about #10932's failing e2e check was passed to the task that owns the PR, and the transcript shows no new task, no question to the user, and no message from the partner. Silence is explicitly allowed here, so this passes. |
| wp-task-wait-ended-ask-zh | zh | **fail** | judge: The reply runs to three sentences, over the one-to-two limit. It also states as fact that the third CI check is still running, but the last readable look only showed two of three done and the third's status is unknown. The task-is-on-it and no-promised-result parts are fine. |
| wp-long-round-ci-zh | zh | pass | judge: The only step is the loop keeper handing the round's facts to the partner, and no message, card, or restart is visible. Silence is an allowed output for this scenario, so the partner did nothing wrong. |
| wp-long-round-stuck-en | en | pass | judge: The partner sent the stuck ci task a clear next step: stop retrying, don't touch credentials, run gh auth status and report. That matches the rubric's pass condition. It did not tell the user anything, which is allowed here, and it did not claim any result. The task message is internal and it avoids repeating the raw error at length. |
| wp-partner-meant-for-other-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner handed the message to 小家 with the partner tool and set no task or reminder itself. It asked nothing and claimed no work was done. The single Chinese line uses 你 and says the message went to 小家, which matches the rubric. |
| wp-partner-other-owns-task-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is in Chinese with 你 and says plainly it can't see the dentist record here. It points to 小家 as the owner of the medical area, doesn't guess whether the appointment is booked, opens no task, and offers to pass the question on. |
| wp-partner-make-zh | zh | pass | judge: The user asked for a second partner named 小家 for home matters. The reply uses 你, is short and direct, and matches the rubric. Minor gap: it doesn't restate what it looks after in the user's own terms beyond '管家里的事', which is close enough to the user's words. |
| wp-partner-pass-back-en | en | pass | judge: The partner relayed the message to Ducter via the partner tool, opened no task here, and replied with one short English line saying it was passed on to Ducter. It claims no answer from Ducter and asks nothing. |
| wp-mem-no-cant-lesson-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner treated the timeout as a one-off: its ack says the page timed out just now and it will retry, with no claim that hel or Codex can't browse. | wp-thread-unnamed-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner answers in two short Chinese sentences, names the race with the index build, the fix of awaiting `indexReady()` before the first query, and the Nov 12 Codex session, all as recall. ## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 저장소 작업, 파일 정리, 웹에서 정보 찾기, 메일이나 일정 확인 같은 일을 함께 할 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 프로젝트에서 실패하는 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 바로 시작할게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 이어서 얘기해요. 편하게 쉬세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は届いたことを確認しながら順番どおりに確実に届けるプロトコルで、UDP は確認や順序保証を省いて速さを優先するプロトコルです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales y actualizar tu rama con los cambios de main antes de compartirla, porque deja un historial lineal y limpio. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la usan otros, porque reescribir commits compartidos rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠、面向连接的传输，保证数据有序送达、丢了会重传；UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared feature branch to keep history linear and tidy it up before review. Merge when the branch is shared with others or when you want the record of how the work came together kept as is.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data files. After a crash, the database replays that log to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones.

### rich-compare-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Put these side by side for me. Mac mini M4: $599, 16 GB, always plugged in, stays at home. MacBook Air M4: $999, 16 GB, runs on battery, comes with me. Which is the better always-on home for you?
  [partner message] **The Mac mini M4.** It costs $400 less, has the same chip and memory, and is built to stay on and plugged in at home.

| | Mac mini M4 | MacBook Air M4 |
|---|---|---|
| Price | $599 | $999 |
| Memory | 16 GB | 16 GB |
| Power | Mains only, runs 24/7 | Battery, must stay plugged in |
| Cooling | Fan, holds speed under long load | Fanless, can slow down under sustained load |
| Ethernet | Gigabit port built in | Needs an adapter |

The Air only makes sense if you need a portable machine. Even then, it would have to stay plugged in with the lid open or run closed on an external display.

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我找到上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细，弄好了告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"。

要做的：
- 找出 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁粒度改细（减少持锁范围或拆分锁，别一把大锁盖住整个 compaction 过程）。
- 跑相关测试，确认 compaction 行为没有变化，并把测试输出作为证据报告。
- 改动提交在你自己的分支上（ducter/compaction-scheduler）。

不要做的：
- 不要 push，不要合并，不要改 PR 的草稿状态。推送和合并等用户点头再说。

完成后报告：改了哪几处锁、为什么这样细化是安全的、测试结果。
  [also used tools] task_find
```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">还没记</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记一笔，记了会列在这里。</div>
</main>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const total = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (Number(x.yuan) || 0), 0);
  const totalEl = document.getElementById("total");
  if (xs.length) { totalEl.textContent = "¥" + total; totalEl.classList.remove("k-none"); }
  else { totalEl.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>'; }
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "";
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden = xs.length > 0;
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? "合计 ¥" + total : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

    var list=todayList(), total=list.reduce(function(s,f){return s+f.ml},0);

  }
  funct … );
      if(openId===f.id){
        var del=document.createElement('button'); del.className='k-row-del'; del.textContent='删除这一条';
        del.onclick=function(ev){ev.stopPropagation(); feeds=feeds.filter(function(x){return x.id!==f.id}); openId=null; save(); render();};
        row.appendChild(del);
      }
      row.onclick=function(){openId=(openId===f.id?null:f.id); render();};
      box.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  function load(v){
    feeds=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];
    render();
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load);
  if(ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(load); });

  document.getElementById('add').onclick=function(){
    var t=document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
    var ml=parseFloat(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if(!/^([01]?\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(t)) return;
    if(!(ml>0)) return;
    var pad=function(t){return t.length===4?'0'+t:t};
    feeds.push({id:Date.now()+Math.random(), d:today(), t:pad(t), ml:ml});
    document.getElementById('t').value='';
    document.getElementById('ml').value='';
    document.querySelector('details.k-add').open=false;
    save(); render();
  };
})();

      row.querySelector(".pr-ref").textContent = it.ref;
      row.querySelector(".pr-status").textContent = it.status;
      function toggle(){
        done[it.id] = !done[it.id];
        if (!done[it.id]) delete done[it.id];
        ducter.set("done", done);
        render();
      }
      row.addEventListener("click", toggle);
      row.addEventListener("keydown", function(e){
        if (e.key === " " || e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); toggle(); }
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    update();
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("done")).then(function(saved){
    done = (saved && typeof saved === "object") ? saved : {};
    render();
  });
  if (typeof ducter.onChange === "function") {
    ducter.onChange(function(){
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get("done")).then(function(saved){
        done = (saved && typeof saved === "object") ? saved : {};
        render();
      });
    });
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#412","status":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","ref":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","status":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","status":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#418","status":"Draft"}]}

<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="餐饮">餐饮</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="其他">其他</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
(function(){
let cat="交通";
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const items=()=>ducter.get("items")||[];
function sum(){return items().reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0)}
function draw(){
  const xs=items(), total … Each(x=>{
    const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=(x.cat||"")+" · "+(x.trip||"");
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=[x.day,x.note].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent="¥"+Number(x.amt||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    r.appendChild(m);r.appendChild(s);r.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(r);
  });
}
function sync(){
  const xs=items();
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+sum().toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
  draw();
}
$("cats").addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest(".k-chip");if(!b)return;
  cat=b.dataset.c;
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false"));
});
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);
  if(!amt||amt<=0)return;
  const xs=items();
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim(),day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:amt,note:""});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";
  sync();
};
ducter.onChange(sync);
sync();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥到北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 我去给 TTL 测试补一条正好到期那一刻的用例，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有的 TTL 假时钟测试基础上，再补一条测试，覆盖条目恰好到期的那一刻：时钟推进到刚好等于 TTL 时，该值应视为已过期（或按现有约定的边界行为，保持与代码一致并在测试里写明）；到期前一刻（差 1 个单位）仍应存在。

完成标准：新测试通过；把 TTL 相关测试连跑多次（如 20 次）全部通过；报告测试输出和改动的文件。

范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。改动留在现有分支上提交即可。

工作目录：~/dev/foo（分支 ducter/foo-ttl）。
```
